function normalizeSavedState() {
  const validPages = new Set(["dashboard","students","dorm","timetable","attendance","scores","admissions","records","programs","alerts","calendar","work","duties","forms","outputs","settings","help"]);
  if (!validPages.has(state.activePage)) state.activePage = "dashboard";
  if (!["mine","homeroom","student","teacher"].includes(state.timetableTab)) state.timetableTab = "mine";
  if (!["month","week"].includes(state.calendarMode)) state.calendarMode = "month";

  state.tasks = Array.isArray(state.tasks) ? state.tasks.filter(Boolean).map(task=>({status:task.done?"done":(task.status||"planned"),...task})) : structuredClone(baseState.tasks);
  ensureDevelopmentTasks();
  state.programEvidence = state.programEvidence && typeof state.programEvidence === "object" ? state.programEvidence : {};
  state.issueReports = Array.isArray(state.issueReports) ? state.issueReports.filter(Boolean) : [];
  state.programAdminReports = state.programAdminReports && typeof state.programAdminReports === "object" ? state.programAdminReports : {};
  state.programOverrides = state.programOverrides && typeof state.programOverrides === "object" ? state.programOverrides : {};
  state.autoAlertAcks = state.autoAlertAcks && typeof state.autoAlertAcks === "object" ? state.autoAlertAcks : {};
  state.programs = (Array.isArray(state.programs) ? state.programs : structuredClone(baseState.programs))
    .filter((program) => program && typeof program === "object")
    .map((program) => ({
      ...program,
      students: (Array.isArray(program.students) ? program.students : []).map((student) => {
        if (student && typeof student === "object")
          return { number: String(student.number || ""), name: String(student.name || "") };
        const [number = "", ...name] = String(student || "").trim().split(/\s+/);
        return { number, name: name.join(" ") };
      }),
    }));

  state.gradeNotices = (Array.isArray(state.gradeNotices) ? state.gradeNotices : structuredClone(baseState.gradeNotices))
    .filter((item) => item && typeof item === "object" && String(item.title || "").trim() !== "방학식 학생 안내 확인")
    .map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      requiredClasses: Array.isArray(item.requiredClasses) ? item.requiredClasses : [],
      confirmedClasses: Array.isArray(item.confirmedClasses) ? item.confirmedClasses : [],
    }));
  state.reportDeadlines = (Array.isArray(state.reportDeadlines) ? state.reportDeadlines : structuredClone(baseState.reportDeadlines)).filter(Boolean);
  state.schoolDeadlines = (Array.isArray(state.schoolDeadlines) ? state.schoolDeadlines : structuredClone(baseState.schoolDeadlines)).filter(Boolean);
  state.dormOutings = (Array.isArray(state.dormOutings) ? state.dormOutings : structuredClone(baseState.dormOutings))
    .filter((item) => item && typeof item === "object")
    .map((item) => ({ date: dateKey(today), ...item }));

  if (!state.settings || typeof state.settings !== "object") state.settings = structuredClone(baseState.settings);
  state.settings.links = { ...baseState.settings.links, ...(state.settings.links && typeof state.settings.links === "object" ? state.settings.links : {}) };

function uepRowsForStudent(map,student){
  const id=String(student?.id||student||'').trim(), no=String(student?.studentNo||'').trim();
  const a=id?(map.get(id)||[]):[], b=no?(map.get(no)||[]):[];
  if(!a.length)return b;if(!b.length||a===b)return a;
  return [...new Set([...a,...b])];
}

function mockExamNames(studentId) {
  const order=["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사","수능"];

function admissionBase(studentId, mockExam = "6월모의고사") {
  const scores=uepRowsForStudent(uepStudentPerfIndex().scoreByStudent,String(studentId));
  const internal=scores.filter(x=>x.scoreType==="내신");
  const preferred=["학기말고사","2차고사","1차고사"].find(exam=>internal.some(x=>x.exam===exam));
  const currentInternal=preferred?internal.filter(x=>x.exam===preferred):internal;
  const gradeRows=currentInternal.filter(x=>{const level=Number(x.level);return Number.isFinite(level)&&level>=1&&level<=5;});
  const creditOf=row=>{const candidates=[row.credit,row.credits,row.courseCredit,row.unitCredit,row['이수학점'],row['이수단위'],row['학점']];for(const v of candidates){const n=Number(v);if(Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0)return n;}return 1;};
  const weightedFive=gradeRows.map(row=>({level:Number(row.level),credit:creditOf(row)}));
  const fiveCredits=weightedFive.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+row.credit,0);
  const avg5=fiveCredits?weightedFive.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+(row.level*row.credit),0)/fiveCredits:null;
  const actual=actualNineAverage(gradeRows);
  const actualDetails=actual.details||[];
  const actualCredits=actualDetails.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+creditOf(item.row||{}),0);
  const actual9=actualCredits?actualDetails.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+(Number(item.result?.grade)*creditOf(item.row||{})),0)/actualCredits:null;
  const mock=scores.filter(x=>x.scoreType==="모의고사"&&x.exam===mockExam);
  return {preferred,currentInternal,avg5,actual9,actualDetails,mock};
}

function minimumCheck(rule, mockRows) {
  const minimumText=String(rule.minimum||"").trim();
  if (minimumText.includes("미적용")) return { result:"미적용", detail:"수능최저 미적용", cls:"" };
  const all=mockRows.filter(row=>row.level!==""&&row.level!==null&&row.level!==undefined).map(row=>({subject:String(row.subject||""),level:Number(row.level)})).filter(item=>Number.isFinite(item.level));
  const english=all.find(item=>item.subject==="영어");
  const history=all.find(item=>item.subject==="한국사");
  const englishLimit=Number(String(rule.english||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]||0);
  const historyLimit=Number(String(rule.history||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]||0);
  if(englishLimit && (!english||english.level>englishLimit)) return {result:"미충족",detail:`영어(${english?.level??"-"}) > 별도기준 ${englishLimit}`,cls:"bad"};
  if(historyLimit && (!history||history.level>historyLimit)) return {result:"미충족",detail:`한국사(${history?.level??"-"}) > 별도기준 ${historyLimit}`,cls:"bad"};
  let count=Number(rule.areaCount||0);
  let sumLimit=Number(rule.sumLimit||0);
  const parsed=minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*(?:개\s*)?(?:영역\s*)?(?:등급\s*)?합\s*(\d+)/)
    || minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*합\s*(\d+)/);
  if(!count&&parsed) count=Number(parsed[1]);
  if(!sumLimit&&parsed) sumLimit=Number(parsed[2]);
  const eachRule=minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*등급\s*(\d+)\s*개/);
  const inquiry=all.filter(item=>!["국어","수학","영어","한국사"].includes(item.subject)).sort((a,b)=>a.level-b.level);
  const core=all.filter(item=>["국어","수학","영어"].includes(item.subject));
  const inquiryCount=Number(rule.inquiryCount||0);
  let candidates=[...core];
  if(inquiryCount===1 && inquiry.length) candidates.push({...inquiry[0],subject:`탐구(${inquiry[0].subject})`});
  else if(inquiryCount>=2) candidates.push(...inquiry.slice(0,inquiryCount).map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));
  else candidates.push(...inquiry.map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));
  candidates=candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.level-b.level);
  if(eachRule){
    const eachLimit=Number(eachRule[1]), eachCount=Number(eachRule[2]);
    const chosen=candidates.slice(0,eachCount);
    if(chosen.length<eachCount) return {result:"자료부족",detail:`${chosen.length}/${eachCount}영역`,cls:"warn"};
    const detail=chosen.map(item=>`${item.subject}(${item.level})`).join(" · ")+` / 각 ${eachLimit}등급 이내`;
    const met=chosen.every(item=>item.level<=eachLimit);
    return {result:met?"충족":"미충족",detail,cls:met?"good":"bad"};
  }
  if(!count||!sumLimit) return {result:"판단보류",detail:minimumText||"세부기준 확인",cls:"warn"};
  const chosen=candidates.slice(0,count);
  if(chosen.length<count) return {result:"자료부족",detail:`${chosen.length}/${count}영역`,cls:"warn"};
  const sum=chosen.reduce((total,item)=>total+item.level,0);
  const detail=`${ch

    state?.growthCounselRecords?.length||0,Object.keys(state?.autoNoticeReceipts||{}).length];
}

  const scores=uepRowsForStudent(uepStudentPerfIndex().scoreByStudent,String(studentId));
  const internal=scores.filter(x=>x.scoreType==="내신");
  const preferred=["학기말고사","2차고사","1차고사"].find(exam=>internal.some(x=>x.exam===exam));
  const currentInternal=preferred?internal.filter(x=>x.exam===preferred):internal;
  const gradeRows=currentInternal.filter(x=>{const level=Number(x.level);return Number.isFinite(level)&&level>=1&&level<=5;});
  const creditOf=row=>{const candidates=[row.credit,row.credits,row.courseCredit,row.unitCredit,row['이수학점'],row['이수단위'],row['학점']];for(const v of candidates){const n=Number(v);if(Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0)return n;}return 1;};
  const weightedFive=gradeRows.map(row=>({level:Number(row.level),credit:creditOf(row)}));
  const fiveCredits=weightedFive.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+row.credit,0);
  const avg5=fiveCredits?weightedFive.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+(row.level*row.credit),0)/fiveCredits:null;
  const actual=actualNineAverage(gradeRows);
  const actualDetails=actual.details||[];
  const actualCredits=actualDetails.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+creditOf(item.row||{}),0);
  const actual9=actualCredits?actualDetails.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+(Number(item.result?.grade)*creditOf(item.row||{})),0)/actualCredits:null;
  const mock=scores.filter(x=>x.scoreType==="모의고사"&&x.exam===mockExam);
  return {preferred,currentInternal,avg5,actual9,actualDetails,mock};
}

function minimumCheck(rule, mockRows) {
  const minimumText=String(rule.minimum||"").trim();
  if (minimumText.includes("미적용")) return { result:"미적용", detail:"수능최저 미적용", cls:"" };
  const all=mockRows.filter(row=>row.level!==""&&row.level!==null&&row.level!==undefined).map(row=>({subject:String(row.subject||""),level:Number(row.level)})).filter(item=>Number.isFinite(item.level));
  const english=all.find(item=>item.subject==="영어");
  const history=all.find(item=>item.subject==="한국사");
  const englishLimit=Number(String(rule.english||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]||0);
  const historyLimit=Number(String(rule.history||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]||0);
  if(englishLimit && (!english||english.level>englishLimit)) return {result:"미충족",detail:`영어(${english?.level??"-"}) > 별도기준 ${englishLimit}`,cls:"bad"};
  if(historyLimit && (!history||history.level>historyLimit)) return {result:"미충족",detail:`한국사(${history?.level??"-"}) > 별도기준 ${historyLimit}`,cls:"bad"};
  let count=Number(rule.areaCount||0);
  let sumLimit=Number(rule.sumLimit||0);
  const parsed=minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*(?:개\s*)?(?:영역\s*)?(?:등급\s*)?합\s*(\d+)/)
    || minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*합\s*(\d+)/);
  if(!count&&parsed) count=Number(parsed[1]);
  if(!sumLimit&&parsed) sumLimit=Number(parsed[2]);
  const eachRule=minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*등급\s*(\d+)\s*개/);
  const inquiry=all.filter(item=>!["국어","수학","영어","한국사"].includes(item.subject)).sort((a,b)=>a.level-b.level);
  const core=all.filter(item=>["국어","수학","영어"].includes(item.subject));
  const inquiryCount=Number(rule.inquiryCount||0);
  let candidates=[...core];
  if(inquiryCount===1 && inquiry.length) candidates.push({...inquiry[0],subject:`탐구(${inquiry[0].subject})`});
  else if(inquiryCount>=2) candidates.push(...inquiry.slice(0,inquiryCount).map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));
  else candidates.push(...inquiry.map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));
  candidates=candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.level-b.level);
  if(eachRule){
    const eachLimit=Number(eachRule[1]), eachCount=Number(eachRule[2]);
    const chosen=candidates.slice(0,eachCount);
    if(chosen.length<eachCount) return {result:"자료부족",detail:`${chosen.length}/${eachCount}영역`,cls:"warn"};
    const detail=chosen.map(item=>`${item.subject}(${item.level})`).join(" · ")+` / 각 ${eachLimit}등급 이내`;
    const met=chosen.every(item=>item.level<=eachLimit);
    return {result:met?"충족":"미충족",detail,cls:met?"good":"bad"};
  }
  if(!count||!sumLimit) return {result:"판단보류",detail:minimumText||"세부기준 확인",cls:"warn"};
  const chosen=candidates.slice(0,count);
  if(chosen.length<count) return {result:"자료부족",detail:`${chosen.length}/${count}영역`,cls:"warn"};
  const sum=chosen.reduce((total,item)=>total+item.level,0);
  const detail=`${chosen.map(item=>`${item.subject}(${item.level})`).join(" + ")} = ${sum} / 기준 ${count}합${sumLimit}${englishLimit?` · 영어≤${englishLimit}`:""}`;
  return {result:sum<=sumLimit?"충족":"미충족",detail,cls:sum<=sumLimit?"good":"bad"};
}

function universityNameKey(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .normalize("NFKC")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\([^)]*\)|\[[^\]]*\]/g,"")
    .replace(/대학교/g,"대")
    .replace(/캠퍼스/g,"")
    .replace(/\s+/g,"")
    .replace(/[()·.\-_/]/g,"");
}

  const detail=unique.map(item=>`${item.subject} ${item.achievement}`).join(" · ");
  return {summary:detail,detail};
}
function internalStatisticsMarkup(exam,classNo){
  const rows=scoreStatisticsRows("internal",exam,classNo);
  if(!exam)return `<div class="query-empty"><b>내신 시험을 선택하세요.</b><span>총점과 평균등급 기준 랭킹을 조회합니다.</span></div>`;
  if(!rows.length)return `<div class="query-empty"><b>${escapeHtml(exam)} 자료가 없습니다.</b><span>선택한 반 또는 성적 원본 자료를 확인하세요.</span></div>`;

  const groups=new Map();
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    if(!groups.has(row.studentId))groups.set(row.studentId,[]);
    groups.get(row.studentId).push(row);
  });
  const results=[...groups.entries()].map(([studentId,items])=>{
    const student=scoreStudentInfo(studentId);
    const gradeRows=items.filter(row=>{
      const level=scoreNumeric(row.level);
      return level!==null&&level>=1&&level<=5;
    });
    const scores=gradeRows.map(row=>scoreNumeric(row.rawScore)).filter(value=>value!==null);
    const levels5=gradeRows.map(row=>scoreNumeric(row.level)).filter(value=>value!==null);
    const actualNine=actualNineAverage(gradeRows);
    const levels9=actualNine.details.map(item=>item.result.grade).filter(value=>Number.isFinite(value));
    const achievement=internalAchievementSummary(items.filter(row=>{
      const level=scoreNumeric(row.level);
      return !(level!==null&&level>=1&&level<=5);
    }));
    return {
      studentId,
      student,
      total:scores.reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0),
      average5:levels5.length?levels5.reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0)/levels5.length:null,
      average9:levels9.length?levels9.reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0)/levels9.length:null,
      gradeSubjects:levels5.length,
      allSubjects:gradeRows.length,
      achievement
    };
  });
  const totalRank=results.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total||((a.average5??99)-(b.average5??99)));
  const averageRank=results.filter(item=>item.average5!==null).slice().sort((a,b)=>a.average5-b.average5||b.total-a.total);
  const totalList=totalRank.map((item,index)=>`<div class="internal-ranking-row total"><strong>${index+1}</strong>${scoreStatisticsStudentButton(item.studentId,`<b>${escapeHtml(item.student.studentNo||"")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name||"")}</span>`)}<span>${item.allSubjects}과목</span><em>${scoreDisplay(item.total,1)}</em><small>평균등급 ${item.average5===null?"-":scoreDisplay(item.average5,2)}(5등급)</small></div>`).join("");
  const averageList=averageRank.map((item,index)=>`<div class="internal-ranking-row average"><strong>${index+1}</strong>${scoreStatisticsStudentButton(item.studentId,`<b>${escapeHtml(item.student.studentNo||"")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name||"")}</span>`)}<span>${item.gradeSubjects}과목</span><em>${scoreDisplay(item.average5,2)}<small>(5등급)</small></em><span class="nine-grade-ref">${item.average9===null?"-":scoreDisplay(item.average9,2)}<small>(9등급 참고)</small></span><span class="achievement-ref" title="${escapeHtml(item.achievement.detail)}">${escapeHtml(item.achievement.summary)}</span><small class="total-ref">${scoreDisplay(item.total,1)}</small></div>`).join("");
  return `<div class="internal-ranking-columns"><section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>${escapeHtml(exam)} 총점 랭킹</h3><p>등급이 산출되는 과목의 원점수 합만 기준으로 정렬합니다. 성취도 A/B/C 과목은 총점에서 제외합니다.</p></div></div><div class="internal-ranking-head total"><span>순위</span><span>학생</span><span>등급산출 과목</span><span>총점</span><span>평균등급<br><small>(5등급)</small></span></div>${totalList}</section><section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>${escapeHtml(exam)} 평균등급 랭킹</h3><p>실제 5등급 평균만 랭킹에 사용합니다. 9등급 참고는 입시 등급환산과 동일하게 과목별 석차/수강자수로 계산합니다.</p></div></div><div class="internal-ranking-head average"><span>순위</span><span>학생</span><span>등급산출 과목</span><span>평균등급<br><small>(5등급)</small></span><span>평균등급<br><small>(9등급 참고)</small></span><span>성취도 과목</span><span>총점</span></div>${averageList}</section></div>`;
}

  let derived=false;

    const peers=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(peer=>
      peer?.scoreType==="내신"&&String(peer.exam||"")===String(row?.exam||"")&&String(peer.subject||"")===String(row?.subject||"")&&
      String(peer.year||"")===String(row?.year||"")&&String(peer.semester||"")===String(row?.semester||"")&&Number.isFinite(scoreNumeric(peer.rawScore))
    );

      if(!Number.isFinite(enrollment)||enrollment<=0)enrollment=peers.length;
      derived=true;
    }
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(rank) || !Number.isFinite(enrollment) || rank <= 0 || enrollment <= 0) return null;
  const percentile = rank / enrollment * 100;
  const grade = percentile <= 4 ? 1 : percentile <= 11 ? 2 : percentile <= 23 ? 3 : percentile <= 40 ? 4 : percentile <= 60 ? 5 : percentile <= 77 ? 6 : percentile <= 89 ? 7 : percentile <= 96 ? 8 : 9;

}
function actualNineAverage(rows) {
  const details = rows.map(row=>({row,result:actualNineGrade(row)})).filter(item=>item.result);
  const average = details.length ? details.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+item.result.grade,0)/details.length : null;
  return { average, details };
}
// v0.76.73 dashboard performance indexes: build once per connected-data generation.
let __uepStudentPerfIndex=null;
function uepStudentPerfIndex(){
  const scores=readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[];
  const programs=readonlyCache?.programs||state.programs||[];
  const reports=readonlyCache?.programReports||readonlyCache?.reports||[];
  const night=readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.nightRecords||[];
  const selected=readonlyCache?.selectedSubjects||[];
  const signature=[scores,programs,reports,night,selected, scores.length,programs.length,reports.length,night.length,selected.length];
  const cached=__uepStudentPerfIndex;
  if(cached&&signature.every((v,i)=>cached.signature[i]===v))return cached;
  const scoreByStudent=new Map(), programByStudent=new Map(), reportByStudent=new Map(), nightByStudent=new Map(), selectedByStudent=new Map();
  const add=(map,key,value)=>{key=String(key||'').trim();if(!key)return;const list=map.get(key);if(list)list.push(value);else map.set(key,[value]);};
  scores.forEach(row=>add(scoreByStudent,row.studentId||row.studentNo,row));
  programs.forEach(program=>(program.students||[]).forEach(st=>{add(programByStudent,st.studentId||st.id,program);add(programByStudent,st.studentNo||st.number,program);}));
  reports.forEach(row=>{add(reportByStudent,row.studentId,row);add(reportByStudent,row.studentNo,row);});
  night.forEach(row=>{add(nightByStudent,row.studentId,row);add(nightByStudent,row.studentNo,row);});
  selected.forEach(row=>{add(selectedByStudent,row.studentId,row);add(selectedByStudent,row.studentNo,row);});
  __uepStudentPerfIndex={signature,scoreByStudent,programByStudent,reportByStudent,nightByStudent,selectedByStudent};
  return __uepStudentPerfIndex;
}
function uepRowsForStudent(map,student){
  const id=String(student?.id||student||'').trim(), no=String(student?.studentNo||'').trim();
  const a=id?(map.get(id)||[]):[], b=no?(map.get(no)||[]):[];
  if(!a.length)return b;if(!b.length||a===b)return a;
  return [...new Set([...a,...b])];
}

function dashboardCounselCacheSignature(){
  const c=readonlyCache||{};
  return [c.students,c.scoreRecords,c.programs,c.programReports,c.reports,c.nightAttendance,c.nightRecords,c.selectedSubjects,c.counselRecords,
    c.students?.length||0,c.scoreRecords?.length||0,c.programs?.length||0,c.programReports?.length||0,c.counselRecords?.length||0,
    state?.growthCounselRecords?.length||0,Object.keys(state?.autoNoticeReceipts||{}).length];
}
function mockExamNames(studentId) {
  const order=["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사","수능"];
  const rows=uepRowsForStudent(uepStudentPerfIndex().scoreByStudent,String(studentId));
  return [...new Set(rows.filter(row=>row.scoreType==="모의고사").map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=order.indexOf(a),bi=order.indexOf(b); return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi)||a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });
}

  const currentInternal=preferred?internal.filter(x=>x.exam===preferred):internal;
  const gradeRows=currentInternal.filter(x=>{const level=Number(x.level);return Number.isFinite(level)&&level>=1&&level<=5;});
  const creditOf=row=>{const candidates=[row.credit,row.credits,row.courseCredit,row.unitCredit,row['이수학점'],row['이수단위'],row['학점']];for(const v of candidates){const n=Number(v);if(Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0)return n;}return 1;};

function minimumCheck(rule, mockRows) {
  const minimumText=String(rule.minimum||"").trim();
  if (minimumText.includes("미적용")) return { result:"미적용", detail:"수능최저 미적용", cls:"" };
  const all=mockRows.filter(row=>row.level!==""&&row.level!==null&&row.level!==undefined).map(row=>({subject:String(row.subject||""),level:Number(row.level)})).filter(item=>Number.isFinite(item.level));
  const english=all.find(item=>item.subject==="영어");
  const history=all.find(item=>item.subject==="한국사");
  const englishLimit=Number(String(rule.english||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]||0);
  const historyLimit=Number(String(rule.history||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]||0);
  if(englishLimit && (!english||english.level>englishLimit)) return {result:"미충족",detail:`영어(${english?.level??"-"}) > 별도기준 ${englishLimit}`,cls:"bad"};
  if(historyLimit && (!history||history.level>historyLimit)) return {result:"미충족",detail:`한국사(${history?.level??"-"}) > 별도기준 ${historyLimit}`,cls:"bad"};
  let count=Number(rule.areaCount||0);
  let sumLimit=Number(rule.sumLimit||0);
  const parsed=minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*(?:개\s*)?(?:영역\s*)?(?:등급\s*)?합\s*(\d+)/)
    || minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*합\s*(\d+)/);
  if(!count&&parsed) count=Number(parsed[1]);
  if(!sumLimit&&parsed) sumLimit=Number(parsed[2]);
  const eachRule=minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*등급\s*(\d+)\s*개/);
  const inquiry=all.filter(item=>!["국어","수학","영어","한국사"].includes(item.subject)).sort((a,b)=>a.level-b.level);
  const core=all.filter(item=>["국어","수학","영어"].includes(item.subject));
  const inquiryCount=Number(rule.inquiryCount||0);
  let candidates=[...core];
  if(inquiryCount===1 && inquiry.length) candidates.push({...inquiry[0],subject:`탐구(${inquiry[0].subject})`});
  else if(inquiryCount>=2) candidates.push(...inquiry.slice(0,inquiryCount).map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));
  else candidates.push(...inquiry.map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));
  candidates=candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.level-b.level);
  if(eachRule){
    const eachLimit=Number(eachRule[1]), eachCount=Number(eachRule[2]);
    const chosen=candidates.slice(0,eachCount);
    if(chosen.length<eachCount) return {result:"자료부족",detail:`${chosen.length}/${eachCount}영역`,cls:"warn"};
    const detail=chosen.map(item=>`${item.subject}(${item.level})`).join(" · ")+` / 각 ${eachLimit}등급 이내`;
    const met=chosen.every(item=>item.level<=eachLimit);
    return {result:met?"충족":"미충족",detail,cls:met?"good":"bad"};
  }
  if(!count||!sumLimit) return {result:"판단보류",detail:minimumText||"세부기준 확인",cls:"warn"};
  const chosen=candidates.slice(0,count);
  if(chosen.length<count) return {result:"자료부족",detail:`${chosen.length}/${count}영역`,cls:"warn"};
  const sum=chosen.reduce((total,item)=>total+item.level,0);
  const detail=`${chosen.map(item=>`${item.subject}(${item.level})`).join(" + ")} = ${sum} / 기준 ${count}합${sumLimit}${englishLimit?` · 영어≤${englishLimit}`:""}`;
  return {result:sum<=sumLimit?"충족":"미충족",detail,cls:sum<=sumLimit?"good":"bad"};
}

function universityNameKey(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .normalize("NFKC")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\([^)]*\)|\[[^\]]*\]/g,"")
    .replace(/대학교/g,"대")
    .replace(/캠퍼스/g,"")
    .replace(/\s+/g,"")
    .replace(/[()·.\-_/]/g,"");
}
function universityMatches(value, query) {
  if (!query) return true;
  const a=universityNameKey(value), b=universityNameKey(query);
  return a.includes(b) || b.includes(a);
}

  return [...new Set([...(readonlyCache?.admissionMinimums||[]).map(x=>x.university),...(readonlyCache?.admissionCuts||[]).map(x=>x.university)].filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
}

  let count=Number(rule.areaCount||0);

    || minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*합\s*(\d+)/);
  if(!count&&parsed) count=Number(parsed[1]);
  if(!sumLimit&&parsed) sumLimit=Number(parsed[2]);

  const core=all.filter(item=>["국어","수학","영어"].includes(item.subject));

  let candidates=[...core];

  else if(inquiryCount>=2) candidates.push(...inquiry.slice(0,inquiryCount).map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));
  else candidates.push(...inquiry.map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));

function admissionBase(studentId, mockExam = "6월모의고사") {
  const scores=uepRowsForStudent(uepStudentPerfIndex().scoreByStudent,String(studentId));
  const internal=scores.filter(x=>x.scoreType==="내신");
  const preferred=["학기말고사","2차고사","1차고사"].find(exam=>internal.some(x=>x.exam===exam));
  const currentInternal=preferred?internal.filter(x=>x.exam===preferred):internal;
  const gradeRows=currentInternal.filter(x=>{const level=Number(x.level);return Number.isFinite(level)&&level>=1&&level<=5;});
  const creditOf=row=>{const candidates=[row.credit,row.credits,row.courseCredit,row.unitCredit,row['이수학점'],row['이수단위'],row['학점']];for(const v of candidates){const n=Number(v);if(Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0)return n;}return 1;};
  const weightedFive=gradeRows.map(row=>({level:Number(row.level),credit:creditOf(row)}));
  const fiveCredits=weightedFive.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+row.credit,0);
  const avg5=fiveCredits?weightedFive.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+(row.level*row.credit),0)/fiveCredits:null;
  const actual=actualNineAverage(gradeRows);
  const actualDetails=actual.details||[];
  const actualCredits=actualDetails.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+creditOf(item.row||{}),0);
  const actual9=actualCredits?actualDetails.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+(Number(item.result?.grade)*creditOf(item.row||{})),0)/actualCredits:null;
  const mock=scores.filter(x=>x.scoreType==="모의고사"&&x.exam===mockExam);
  return {preferred,currentInternal,avg5,actual9,actualDetails,mock};
}

function minimumCheck(rule, mockRows) {
  const minimumText=String(rule.minimum||"").trim();
  if (minimumText.includes("미적용")) return { result:"미적용", detail:"수능최저 미적용", cls:"" };
  const all=mockRows.filter(row=>row.level!==""&&row.level!==null&&row.level!==undefined).map(row=>({subject:String(row.subject||""),level:Number(row.level)})).filter(item=>Number.isFinite(item.level));
  const english=all.find(item=>item.subject==="영어");
  const history=all.find(item=>item.subject==="한국사");
  const englishLimit=Number(String(rule.english||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]||0);
  const historyLimit=Number(String(rule.history||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]||0);
  if(englishLimit && (!english||english.level>englishLimit)) return {result:"미충족",detail:`영어(${english?.level??"-"}) > 별도기준 ${englishLimit}`,cls:"bad"};
  if(historyLimit && (!history||history.level>historyLimit)) return {result:"미충족",detail:`한국사(${history?.level??"-"}) > 별도기준 ${historyLimit}`,cls:"bad"};
  let count=Number(rule.areaCount||0);
  let sumLimit=Number(rule.sumLimit||0);
  const parsed=minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*(?:개\s*)?(?:영역\s*)?(?:등급\s*)?합\s*(\d+)/)
    || minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*합\s*(\d+)/);
  if(!count&&parsed) count=Number(parsed[1]);
  if(!sumLimit&&parsed) sumLimit=Number(parsed[2]);
  const eachRule=minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*등급\s*(\d+)\s*개/);
  const inquiry=all.filter(item=>!["국어","수학","영어","한국사"].includes(item.subject)).sort((a,b)=>a.level-b.level);
  const core=all.filter(item=>["국어","수학","영어"].includes(item.subject));
  const inquiryCount=Number(rule.inquiryCount||0);
  let candidates=[...core];
  if(inquiryCount===1 && inquiry.length) candidates.push({...inquiry[0],subject:`탐구(${inquiry[0].subject})`});
  else if(inquiryCount>=2) candidates.push(...inquiry.slice(0,inquiryCount).map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));
  else candidates.push(...inquiry.map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));
  candidates=candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.level-b.level);
  if(eachRule){
    const eachLimit=Number(eachRule[1]), eachCount=Number(eachRule[2]);
    const chosen=candidates.slice(0,eachCount);
    if(chosen.length<eachCount) return {result:"자료부족",detail:`${chosen.length}/${eachCount}영역`,cls:"warn"};
    const detail=chosen.map(item=>`${item.subject}(${item.level})`).join(" · ")+` / 각 ${eachLimit}등급 이내`;
    const met=chosen.every(item=>item.level<=eachLimit);
    return {result:met?"충족":"미충족",detail,cls:met?"good":"bad"};
  }
  if(!count||!sumLimit) return {result:"판단보류",detail:minimumText||"세부기준 확인",cls:"warn"};
  const chosen=candidates.slice(0,count);
  if(chosen.length<count) return {result:"자료부족",detail:`${chosen.length}/${count}영역`,cls:"warn"};
  const sum=chosen.reduce((total,item)=>total+item.level,0);
  const detail=`${chosen.map(item=>`${item.subject}(${item.level})`).join(" + ")} = ${sum} / 기준 ${count}합${sumLimit}${englishLimit?` · 영어≤${englishLimit}`:""}`;
  return {result:sum<=sumLimit?"충족":"미충족",detail,cls:sum<=sumLimit?"good":"bad"};
}

function universityNameKey(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .normalize("NFKC")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\([^)]*\)|\[[^\]]*\]/g,"")
    .replace(/대학교/g,"대")
    .replace(/캠퍼스/g,"")
    .replace(/\s+/g,"")
    .replace(/[()·.\-_/]/g,"");
}
function universityMatches(value, query) {
  if (!query) return true;
  const a=universityNameKey(value), b=universityNameKey(query);
  return a.includes(b) || b.includes(a);
}

}

// 학생정보 상담·학사생·입시·성장관리센터·세특 검수가 같은 기준을 공유합니다.
const UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES=Object.freeze({
  inseoul:Object.freeze({key:'inseoul',label:'인서울',groupLabel:'인서울 관리군',internal:2.5,boundary:0.35,
    mockText:'국·수·영 중 2개 영역 2등급 이내, 나머지 3등급 이내'}),
  regional:Object.freeze({key:'regional',label:'지거국',groupLabel:'지거국 관리군',internal:4.0,boundary:0.35,
    mockText:'국·수·영 중 2개 영역 4등급 이내, 전체 5등급 이내'}),
  general:Object.freeze({key:'general',label:'일반',groupLabel:'일반 관리군',internal:null,boundary:0.35,
    mockText:'최근 모의 성적의 상승 흐름 확인'})
});
function uepManagementRule(group='general'){
  return UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES[group]||UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.general;
}
let __uepManagementFiveGroupCache=null;
function uepManagementFiveGroupKey(avg5){
  const value=Number(avg5);
  return Number.isFinite(value)?value.toFixed(2):null;
}
function uepManagementExpandedFiveGroups(){
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[], scores=readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[];
  const signature=[students,scores,students.length,scores.length];
  const cached=__uepManagementFiveGroupCache;
  if(cached&&signature.every((v,i)=>cached.signature[i]===v))return cached;
  const rows=students.filter(student=>String(student.status||'재학')==='재학').map(student=>{
    const base=admissionBase(student.id);
    return {actual9:Number(base.actual9),fiveKey:uepManagementFiveGroupKey(base.avg5)};
  });
  const inseoul=new Set(rows.filter(row=>Number.isFinite(row.actual9)&&row.actual9<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.inseoul.internal&&row.fiveKey).map(row=>row.fiveKey));
  const regional=new Set(rows.filter(row=>Number.isFinite(row.actual9)&&row.actual9<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.regional.internal&&row.fiveKey).map(row=>row.fiveKey));
  __uepManagementFiveGroupCache={signature,inseoul,regional};
  return __uepManagementFiveGroupCache;
}
function uepBaseManagementGroup(actual9,avg5=null){
  const value=Number(actual9),fiveKey=uepManagementFiveGroupKey(avg5);
  if(!Number.isFinite(value))return 'general';
  const expanded=fiveKey?uepManagementExpandedFiveGroups():null;
  if(value<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.inseoul.internal||(expanded&&expanded.inseoul.has(fiveKey)))return 'inseoul';
  if(value<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.regional.internal||(expanded&&expanded.regional.has(fiveKey)))return 'regional';
  return 'general';
}
function uepMockCoreFor(group,mock={}){
  if(group==='inseoul')return mock.mainLevels?.length>=3&&Number(mock.mainWithin2||0)>=2&&mock.mainLevels.every(v=>v<=3);
  if(group==='regional')return Number(mock.mainWithin4||0)>=2&&Boolean(mock.allWithin5);
  return false;
}
function uepMockPotentialFor(group,mock={}){
  if(group==='inseoul')return mock.mainLevels?.length>=3&&Number(mock.mainWithin3||0)>=2&&mock.mainLevels.every(v=>v<=4);
  if(group==='regional')return mock.mainLevels?.length>=3&&Number(mock.mainWithin4||0)>=1&&mock.mainLevels.every(v=>v<=5);
  return false;
}
function uepManagementStage(internalOk,mockCore,mockPotential){
  if(internalOk&&mockCore)return 'core';
  if(internalOk&&mockPotential)return 'growth';
  if(internalOk||mockCore)return 'interest';
  return 'general';
}
function studentCounselStatusLabel(type){
  return type==='ok'?'충족':type==='near'?'근접':'부족';
}
function studentCounselStatusClass(type){
  return type==='ok'?'ok':type==='near'?'near':'bad';

function studentCounselCriteria(candidate,groupKey){
  const base=candidate?.base||{}, mock=candidate?.mock||{}, prep=candidate?.prep||{};
  const actual=Number(base.actual9), hasActual=Number.isFinite(actual);
  const internalLimit=groupKey==='seoul'?uepManagementRule('inseoul').internal:groupKey==='regional'?uepManagementRule('regional').internal:null;
  let internal='bad';
  const expandedGroup=hasActual?uepBaseManagementGroup(actual,base.avg5):'general';
  const expandedOk=groupKey==='seoul'?expandedGroup==='inseoul':groupKey==='regional'?['inseoul','regional'].includes(expandedGroup):false;
  if(internalLimit==null) internal=hasActual&&actual<=4.5?'near':'bad';
  else if(expandedOk) internal='ok';
  else if(hasActual&&actual<=internalLimit+0.35) internal='near';
  const seoulMock=uepMockCoreFor('inseoul',mock);
  const regionalMock=uepMockCoreFor('regional',mock);
  let mockState=groupKey==='seoul'?(seoulMock?'ok':Number(mock.mainWithin2||0)>=1?'near':'bad'):
    groupKey==='regional'?(regionalMock?'ok':Number(mock.mainWithin4||0)>=1?'near':'bad'):
    (mock.levels?.length?(Number(mock.best||99)<=4?'near':'bad'):'bad');
  const subjectState=Number(prep.subjects||0)>0?'ok':'bad';
  const activityTotal=Number(prep.selected||0)+Number(prep.reports||0);
  const activityState=activityTotal>=2?'ok':activityTotal===1?'near':'bad';
  const reportState=Number(prep.reports||0)>=2?'ok':Number(prep.reports||0)===1?'near':'bad';
  return {internal,mock:mockState,subjects:subjectState,activity:activityState,reports:reportState,internalLimit};
}

function dormTermRoster(mode){
  const term=dormTermDefinition(mode);if(!term)return [];
  const all=[...(readonlyCache?.dormHistory||[]),...(readonlyCache?.pastDormStudents||[]),...(readonlyCache?.dormStudents||[])];
  const seen=new Set();
  return all.filter(row=>{
    const year=String(row.year||'').replace(/\.0$/,'');
    const sem=String(row.semester||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');
    const no=String(row.studentNo||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
    const inferredGrade=no?Number(no[0]):Number(String(row.className||'').match(/^([123])[- ]/)?.[1]||1);
    const grade=Number(row.grade||inferredGrade||1);
    if(year&&term.year&&year!==term.year)return false;
    if(grade!==term.grade)return false;
    if(term.semester&&sem&&sem!==term.semester)return false;
    if(term.semester&&!sem){
      const start=String(row.startDate||'');
      if(start){const month=Number(start.slice(5,7));if(term.semester==='1'&&month>=8)return false;if(term.semester==='2'&&month>0&&month<7)return false;}
    }
    const key=`${row.studentId||row.studentNo}|${term.label}`;if(seen.has(key))return false;seen.add(key);return true;
  }).sort(dormStudentNoSort);
}
function dormScoreRows(studentId){return (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(row=>String(row.studentId||'')===String(studentId||''));}
function dormAverage(rows){const values=rows.map(row=>Number(row.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);return values.length?values.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/values.length:null;}
function dormInternalTrend(student,term){
  const rows=dormScoreRows(student.id).filter(row=>row.scoreType==='내신');
  const terms=[['1-1','2026','1'],['1-2','2026','2'],['2-1','2027','1'],['2-2','2027','2'],['3','2028','']];
  const limit=term.grade===3?4:(term.grade-1)*2+(term.semester==='2'?1:0);
  return terms.slice(0,limit+1).map(([label,year,semester])=>({label,value:dormAverage(rows.filter(row=>(!row.schoolYear||String(row.schoolYear)===year)&&(!semester||!row.semester||String(row.semester).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'')===semester)))})).filter(x=>x.value!=null);
}
function dormMockTrend(student,term){
  const order=['3월모의고사','6월모의고사','9월모의고사','10월모의고사','11월모의고사','수능'];
  const rows=dormScoreRows(student.id).filter(row=>row.scoreType==='모의고사');
  const years=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>String(row.schoolYear||'')).filter(Boolean))].sort();
  const allowedYears=years.filter(year=>Number(year)<=Number(term.year));
  const result=[];
  allowedYears.forEach(year=>order.forEach(exam=>{const avg=dormAverage(rows.filter(row=>String(row.schoolYear||'')===year&&row.exam===exam));if(avg!=null)result.push({label:`${year.slice(-2)}.${exam.replace('모의고사','').replace('월','')}`,value:avg});}));
  return result.slice(-5);
}
function dormSparkline(points){
  if(!points.length)return '<span class="dorm-spark-empty">자료 대기</span>';
  const w=118,h=30,p=4,vals=points.map(x=>x.value),min=Math.min(...vals),max=Math.max(...vals),range=Math.max(.4,max-min);
  if(points.length===1){
    return `<svg class="dorm-spark dorm-spark-one" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="${w/2}" cy="${h/2}" r="3.2"/></svg>`;
  }
  const coords=points.map((x,i)=>`${p+i*(w-p*2)/(points.length-1)},${p+(x.value-min)*(h-p*2)/range}`).join(' ');
  const dots=coords.split(' ').map(pair=>{const [x,y]=pair.split(',');return `<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="2.1"/>`;}).join('');
  return `<svg class="dorm-spark" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="${coords}" fill="none" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>${dots}</svg>`;
}
function dormManagementBadge(student){
  const candidate=counselPriorityCandidateForStudent(student);if(!candidate)return {label:'',reason:'안정 추적',candidate:null};
  const score=Number(candidate.score||0);const rank=score>=110?'관리 우선 ①':score>=80?'관리 우선 ②':'관리 확인';
  return {label:rank,reason:(candidate.tags||candidate.reasons||[])[0]||'상담 확인',candidate};
}
function dormAdmissionLine(student){
  const rows=studentAdmissionStageRows(student)||[];
  const inseoul=rows.find(x=>x.group==='inseoul'),regional=rows.find(x=>x.group==='regional');
  const label=x=>!x?'일반':x.stage==='core'?'핵심':x.stage==='growth'?'성장':x.stage==='interest'?'관심':'일반';
  return `인서울 ${label(inseoul)} · 지거국 ${label(regional)}`;
}

function dormAxisChart(points,maxGrade=9){
  if(!points.length)return '<div class="dorm-axis-empty">연결된 성적 자료가 없습니다.</div>';
  const w=620,h=132,left=48,right=18,top=14,bottom=30,plotW=w-left-right,plotH=h-top-bottom;
  const max=Math.max(2,Number(maxGrade)||9), minGrade=1;
  const x=(i)=>points.length===1?left+plotW/2:left+i*plotW/(points.length-1);
  const y=(v)=>top+(Math.max(minGrade,Math.min(max,Number(v)))-minGrade)*plotH/(max-minGrade);
  const actualValues=[...new Set(points.map(p=>Number(p.value)).filter(Number.isFinite).map(v=>Number(v.toFixed(2))))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const grid=actualValues.map(value=>{const yy=y(value);return `<line x1="${left}" y1="${yy}" x2="${w-right}" y2="${yy}" class="grid actual"/><text x="${left-8}" y="${yy+3}" text-anchor="end">${value.toFixed(value%1?2:0)}</text>`;}).join('');
  const path=points.length>1?`<polyline points="${points.map((p,i)=>`${x(i)},${y(p.value)}`).join(' ')}" class="line"/>`:'';
  const dots=points.map((p,i)=>`<circle cx="${x(i)}" cy="${y(p.value)}" r="4" class="dot"/><text x="${x(i)}" y="${Math.max(10,y(p.value)-8)}" text-anchor="middle" class="value-label">${Number(p.

career","진로활동 특기사항"],["behavior","종합의견(행동발달 특기사항)"]];
    body+=areas.map(([area,label])=>{const draft=localStorage.getItem(recordDraftKey(student.id,area))||"";const final=existingFinalRecord(bundle,area);return `<section class="print-section"><h2>${label}</h2><h3>초안 · ${draft.length}자</h3><div class="record-box draft">${escapeHtml(draft||"저장된 초안 없음")}</div><h3>최종본 · ${(final?.text||"").length}자</h3><div class="record-box final">${escapeHtml(final?.text||"확정된 최종본 없음")}</div></section>`}).join("");
  }
  openPrintDocument(`${student.studentNo}_${student.name}_생활기록부`,body);
}

function printScorePackage(student,mode){
  if(!student)return;
  const all=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.studentId===student.id);
  const internal=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==="내신");
  const mock=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==="모의고사");
  const gradeRows=internal.filter(r=>Number.isFinite(Number(r.level)));
  const avg=gradeRows.length?(gradeRows.reduce((s,r)=>s+Number(r.level),0)/gradeRows.length).toFixed(2):"-";
  const mockExams=[...new Set(mock.map(r=>r.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b));
  const latestMock=mockExams.at(-1)||"-";
  const subjects=[...new Set(mock.map(r=>r.subject).filter(Boolean))];
  const summary=`<div class="score-print-summary"><div><small>최근 내신 평균</small><b>${avg}</b><span>등급 평균</span></div><div><small>내신 자료</small><b>${internal.length}</b><span>과목·시험 기록</span></div><div><small>최근 모의고사</small><b>${escapeHtml(latestMock)}</b><span>${mock.length}개 성적</span></div><div><small>조회범위</small><b>${mode==="combined"?"종합":mode==="mock"?"모의":"내신"}</b><span>개인별 누적</span></div></div>`;
  const internalExams=[...new Set(internal.map(r=>r.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b));
  const internalSubjects=[...new Set(internal.map(r=>r.subject).filter(Boolean))];
  const internalTable=`<section class="print-section"><h2>내신 누적 성적</h2><table class="score-wide"><thead><tr><th>과목</th>${internalExams.map(e=>`<th>${escapeHtml(e)}<small>등급 / 원점수</small></th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${internalSubjects.map(subject=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(subject)}</td>${internalExams.map(exam=>{const r=internal.find(x=>x.subject===subject&&x.exam===exam);return `<td>${r?`<b>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.level))}등급</b><small>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.rawScore))}점 · ${escapeHtml(r.achievement||"")}</small>`:"-"}</td>`}).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section>`;
  const mockTable=`<section class="print-section"><h2>모의고사 비교</h2><table class="score-wide mock"><thead><tr><th>영역</th>${mockExams.map(e=>`<th>${escapeHtml(e)}<small>등급 / 원점수 / 표준</small></th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${subjects.map(subject=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(subject)}</td>${mockExams.map(exam=>{const r=mock.find(x=>x.subject===subject&&x.exam===exam);return `<td>${r?`<b>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.level))}등급</b><small>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.rawScore))}점 · 표준 ${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.standardScore))}${r.percentile!==""?` · 백분위 ${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.percentile))}`:""}</small>`:"-"}</td>`}).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section>`;
  const body=`<header class="print-head score-title"><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM · 학생상담 성적 종합</small><h1>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</h1><p>진로희망: ${escapeHtml(student.career||"-")} · 전체 누적 성적</p>${studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student)}</header>${summary}${mode!=="mock"?internalTable:""}${mode!=="internal"?mockTable:""}`;
  openPrintDocument(`${student.studentNo}_${student.name}_성적상담자료`,body,"portrait");
}

function availableClasses() {
  const found = [...new Set((readonlyCache?.students || []).map(classNumberOf).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));
  return found.length ? found : Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1));
}
function optionStudents(classNo, selectedId = "") {
  return (readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => !classNo || classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s => `<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${s.id === selectedId ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(s.name)}</option>`).join("");
}

function scorePeriodKey(row) {
  const year = String(row?.schoolYear || today.getFullYear()).replace(/[^0-9]/g, "") || String(today.getFullYear());
  let semester = String(row?.semester || "").match(/[12]/)?.[0] || "";
  if (!semester && row?.scoreType === "모의고사") {
    const month = Number(String(row.exam || "").match(/(\d{1,2})\s*월/)?.[1] || 0);
    semester = month && month >= 8 ? "2" : "1";
  }
  return `${year}-${semester || "1"}`;
}
function scorePeriodLabel(key) {
  if (!key || key === "all") return "전체 누적";
  const [year, semester] = String(key).split("-");
  return `${year}학년도 ${semester || "1"}학기`;
}
function availableScorePeriods(mode = scoreMode) {
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => mode === "combined" || row.scoreType === (mode === "mock" ? "모의고사" : "내신"));
  return [...new Set(rows.map(scorePeriodKey).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
}
function scoreExamOrder(exam) {
  const order = ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사","3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사","수능"];
  const index = order.indexOf(exam);
  return index < 0 ? 999 : index;
}
function scoreTrend(row, allRows) {
  const orders = row.scoreType === "모의고사" ? ["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사"] : ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사"];
  const index = orders.indexOf(row.exam);
  if (index <= 0 || row.level === "" || row.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
  const previous = allRows.find(x => x.studentId === row.studentId && x.scoreType === row.scoreType && x.subject === row.subject && x.exam === orders[index - 1] && scorePeriodKey(x) === period);
  if (!previous || previous.level === "" || previous.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const diff = Number(previous.level) - Number(row.level);
  if (!Number.isFinite(diff) || diff === 0) return { text:"유지", cls:"" };
  return diff > 0 ? { text:`▲ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"good" } : { text:`▼ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"bad" };
}
function gradeToneClass(value) {
  const grade = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(grade) && grade >= 1 && grade <= 9 ? `grade-${grade}` : "grade-na";
}
function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">-</span>`;
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span>`;
  return `<span class="grade-badge ${gradeToneClass(number)}"><b>${scoreDisplay(number,2)}</b><small>${suffix}</small></span>`;
}
function mockGradeMiniMarkup(studentId, exam) {
  const order = ["국어", "수학", "영어", "한국사"];
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.studentId === studentId && row.scoreType === "모의고사" && row.exam === exam);
  if (!rows.length) return `<span class="mock-grade-empty">자료 없음</span>`;
  const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=order.indexOf(a.subject), bi=order.indexOf(b.subject);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
    return String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),"ko");
  });
  return `<div class="mock-grade-mini-strip">${sorted.map(row=>`<span><small>${escapeHtml(row.subject)}</small><b class="${gradeToneClass(row.level)}">${escapeHtml(String(row.level || "-"))}</b></span>`).join("")}</div>`;
}
function scoreTableMarkup(rows, mode = scoreMode) {
  const coreOrder = mode === "mock" ? ["국어","수학","영어","한국사"] : ["공통국어1","공통수학1","공통영어1","한국사1","통합사회1","통합과학1","한문","과학탐구실험1","체육1","음악"];
  const subjects = [...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=coreOrder.indexOf(a), bi=coreOrder.indexOf(b);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi);
    return a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });
  const groups = new Map();
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
    const key=`${row.studentId}|${period}|${row.exam}`;
    if(!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key,{studentId:row.studentId,studentNo:row.studentNo,name:row.name,period,exam:row.exam,items:new Map()});
    groups.get(key).items.set(row.subject,row);
  });
  const grouped=[...groups.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.studentNo)) || a.period.localeCompare(b.period) || scoreExamOrder(a.exam)-scoreExamOrder(b.exam) || String(a.exam).localeCompare(String(b.exam),"ko"));
  const cell=(row)=>{
    if(!row) return `<td class="empty-score-cell">-</td>`;
    const trend=scoreTrend(row,readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]);
    const trendMarkup=trend.text!=="-"?`<em class="trend ${trend.cls}">${trend.text}</em>`:"";
    if(row.scoreType==="모의고사") return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(row.level)}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 표준 ${scoreDisplay(row.standardScore)}${row.percentile!==""?` · 백분위 ${scoreDisplay(row.percentile)}`:""}

  const coreOrder = mode === "mock" ? ["국어","수학","영어","한국사"] : ["공통국어1","공통수학1","공통영어1","한국사1","통합사회1","통합과학1","한문","과학탐구실험1","체육1","음악"];

    return a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });

    const gradeValue = row.level === "산출제외" ? (row.achievement || "산출제외") : row.level;
    return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(gradeValue, Number.isFinite(Number(gradeValue))?"등급":"")}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 석차 ${escapeHtml(String(row.rank||"-"))}/${escapeHtml(String(row.enrollment||"-"))}${row.achievement?` · ${escapeHtml(row.achievement)}`:""}</small></td>`;
  };

}

function scoreDisplay(value, digits = 1) {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return "-";
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return escapeHtml(String(value));
  return Number.isInteger(number) ? String(number) : number.toFixed(digits).replace(/\.0+$/, "");
}
function scoreAverage(rows) {
  const values = rows.filter((row) => row.level !== "" && row.level !== null && row.level !== undefined).map((row) => Number(row.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);
  return values.length ? values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length : null;
}

function scoreDisplay(value, digits = 1) {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return "-";
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return escapeHtml(String(value));
  return Number.isInteger(number) ? String(number) : number.toFixed(digits).replace(/\.0+$/, "");
}

}

function runScoreQuery() {
  const scope = $("#scoreScope")?.value || "class";
  const classNo = $("#scoreClass")?.value || "";
  const studentId = $("#scoreStudent")?.value || "";
  const exam = $("#scoreExam")?.value || "";
  const target = $("#scoreQueryResult");
  let rows = readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || [];
  if (scoreMode === "internal") rows = rows.filter(x => x.scoreType === "내신");
  if (scoreMode === "mock") rows = rows.filter(x => x.scoreType === "모의고사");
  if (scorePeriod !== "all") rows = rows.filter(x=>scorePeriodKey(x)===scorePeriod);
  if (scope === "individual") {
    if (!studentId) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>학생을 선택하세요.</b><span>반을 먼저 선택한 뒤 학생을 고르고 조회하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
    rows = rows.filter(x => x.studentId === studentId);
  } else {
    if (!classNo) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>반을 선택하세요.</b><span>반을 선택한 뒤 조회 버튼을 눌러야 성적이 표시됩니다.</span></div>`; return; }
    const ids = new Set((readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s=>s.id));
    rows = rows.filter(x => ids.has(x.studentId));
  }
  if (scoreMode === "combined" && scope !== "individual") { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>종합성적은 개인별로 조회하세요.</b><span>반과 학생을 선택하면 학사상담 대시보드가 표시됩니다.</span></div>`; return; }
  if (scoreMode === "combined" && scope === "individual") {
    const student = (readonlyCache?.students || []).find(item=>item.id===studentId);
    if (!student) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>학생자료를 찾지 못했습니다.</b><span>학생정보 연결 상태를 확인하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
    target.innerHTML = combinedScoreDashboardMarkup(student, rows); return;
  }
  if (exam) rows = rows.filter(x=>x.exam===exam);
  if (!rows.length) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>조회 결과가 없습니다.</b><span>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod))}의 반·시험 자료를 확인하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
  let averageRows=rows, averageLabel='5등급제 평균';
  if(scoreMode==='internal'&&scope==='individual'){
    const periods=[...new Set(rows.map(scorePeriodKey).filter(Boolean))].sort();
    const latestPeriod=periods.at(-1)||'';
    const periodRows=latestPeriod?rows.filter(row=>scorePeriodKey(row)===latestPeriod):rows;
    const preferredExam=['학기말고사','2차고사','1차고사'].find(name=>periodRows.some(row=>row.exam===name));
    averageRows=preferredExam?periodRows.filter(row=>row.exam===preferredExam):periodRows;
    averageLabel=preferredExam==='학기말고사'?'최근 학기말 평균':preferredExam?`최근 ${preferredExam} 임시평균`:'5등급제 평균';
  }
  const gradeValues=averageRows.map(x=>Number(x.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);
  const average=gradeValues.length?(gradeValues.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/gradeValues.length).toFixed(2):"-";
  const persons=new Set(rows.map(x=>x.studentId)).size, subjects=new Set(rows.map(x=>x.subject).filter(Boolean)).size;
  const modeLabel={internal:"내신",mock:"모의고사",combined:"종합"}[scoreMode]||"종합";
  const third=scoreMode==="mock"?`<span><b>${subjects}</b>과목별 등급</span>`:`<span><b>${average}</b>${escapeHtml(averageLabel)}</span>`;
  target.innerHTML=`<div class="query-summary"><span><b>${persons}</b>명</span><span><b>${rows.length}</b>성적행</span>${third}<span><b>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod))}</b>조회범위</span></div>${scoreTableMarkup(rows,scoreMode)}`;
}

function scoreNumeric(value){
  const number=Number(String(value??"").replace(/[^0-9.\-]/g,""));

function scoreStatisticsRows(type,exam,classNo="all"){
  const wanted=type==="mock"?"모의고사":"내신";
  let rows=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(row=>row.scoreType===wanted&&(!exam||row.exam===exam));
  if(classNo!=="all"){
    const ids=new Set((readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(student=>classNumberOf(student)===String(classNo)).map(student=>student.id));
    rows=rows.filter(row=>ids.has(row.studentId));
  }
  return rows;
}

/* UEP_08225_ADMISSION_CALCULATION_CARDS */
(function(){
  const original=openDashboardUniversityDetail;
  openDashboardUniversityDetail=function(university=dashboardAdmissionTodayUniversity()){
    const ret=original(university);
    if(!university)return ret;
    const norm=dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(university['대학명']||'');
    const calcs=dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionGradeCalcs','57_내신산정DB').filter(r=>dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(r['대학명'])===norm);
    const minimums=dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionMinimums','54_수능최저DB').filter(r=>dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(r['대학명'])===norm&&String(r['수능최저원문']||'').trim());
    const calc=calcs.find(r=>String(r['검증상태']||'').startsWith('A-'))||calcs[0]||null;
    const esc=v=>escapeHtml(String(v??''));
    const line=(label,value)=>value?'<div class="uep-uni-detail-line"><b>'+esc(label)+'</b><span>'+esc(value)+'</span></div>':'';
    const calcHtml=calc?
      line('반영학년',calc['반영학년'])+
      line('반영교과·과목',calc['반영교과/과목'])+
      line('등급점수',calc['석차등급배점'])+
      line('성취도점수',calc['성취도배점/환산'])+
      line('등급 미기재',calc['석차등급미기재과목처리'])+
      line('이수학점·가중',calc['이수학점가중'])+
      line('최종 산식',calc['내신산식']):
      '<div class="uep-uni-detail-pending"><b>정확한 숫자 산식 검증중</b><span>등급별 점수·성취도별 점수·가중치가 공식 원문에서 확인된 뒤 표시합니다.</span></div>';
    const minHtml=minimums.length?minimums.slice(0,10).map(r=>'<div class="uep-uni-min-row"><b>'+esc(r['모집단위']||r['전형명']||'모집단위')+'</b><span>'+esc(r['수능최저원문'])+'</span></div>').join(''):'<div class="uep-uni-detail-pending"><b>모집단위별 기준 검증중</b><span>공식 원문 숫자가 확인된 기준만 표시합니다.</span></div>';
    const course=String(university['과목선택/교과포인트']||'').trim();
    const courseHtml=course?'<div class="uep-uni-course-copy">'+esc(course)+'</div>':'<div class="uep-uni-detail-pending"><b>공식 권장과목 확인중</b><span>대학이 공식적으로 제시한 권장·핵심·관련 과목만 표시합니다.</span></div>';
    const section=document.querySelector('.uep-uni-summary-section');
    if(section){section.innerHTML=
      '<div class="uep-uni-summary-card uep-uni-calc-card"><small>내신성적 산출방법</small><div class="uep-uni-detail-stack">'+calcHtml+'</div></div>'+
      '<div class="uep-uni-summary-card uep-uni-minimum-card"><small>모집단위별 수능최저</small><div class="uep-uni-detail-stack">'+minHtml+'</div></div>'+
      '<div class="uep-uni-summary-card uep-uni-course-card"><small>권장과목</small><div class="uep-uni-detail-stack">'+courseHtml+'</div></div>';
    }
    return ret;
  };
})();

let uepSchoolReadDiagnostics = [];
function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
  if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
  if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
  if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
  if(typeof value==='object'){
    const out={};
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
      if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
      out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return String(value);
}
function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}
async function downloadSchoolReadDiagnostics(){
  let verified=null;
  try{verified=await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus?.({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify',verified);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  const report={
    schema:'uep-school-read-diagnostic-v1',
    appVersion:APP_VERSION,
    exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    platform:navigator.platform,
    online:navigator.onLine,
    schoolReadBridge:{
      sessionStatus:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus),
      login:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin),
      preview:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync),
      readCache:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)
    },
    googleConnectionStatus:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionStatus),
    googleConnectionError:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionError),
    cacheSummary:{
      syncedAt:readonlyCache?.syncedAt||null,
      students:readonlyCache?.students?.length||0,
      dormStudents:readonlyCache?.dormStudents?.length||0
    },
    verifiedSession:uepDiagSafe(verified),
    events:uepSchoolReadDiagnostics
  };
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(report,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;
  a.download='UEP-school-read-diagnostic-'+new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-')+'.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),2000);
  toast('진단 JSON 파일을 저장했습니다. 이 파일을 채팅에 올려 주세요.');
}

        .catch((error) => {googleConnectionStatus={ok:false,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};googleConnectionError=error?.message||'학교 공용 읽기 연결 실패';console.warn("[UEP] School Read API 최초 연결 실패", error);})
    : Promise.resolve();
  const cacheTask = window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache
    ? withStartupTimeout(window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache(), 8000, null)
        .then((cached) => {
          if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
          else if (cached) googleConnectionError = cached.reason || "학교 데이터 동기화를 확인하세요. 마지막 저장 자료는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.";
        })
        .catch((error) => {
          console.warn("[UEP] 읽기 캐시 불러오기 실패", error);
          googleConnectionError = error?.message || "학교 데이터 동기화를 확인하세요. 마지막 저장 자료는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.";
        })
    : Promise.resolve();
  await Promise.all([credentialTask, cacheTask]);
  // 화면은 로컬/메모리 캐시로 먼저 표시한 뒤, 실행당 한 번만 백그라운드 원격 동기화한다.
  // 사용자는 기다리지 않고 UEP를 사용하며, 최신 Google Sheet가 도착하면 현재 화면만 갱신한다.
  if(googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const fullSyncedAt=readonlyCache?.syncedAt?new Date(readonlyCache.syncedAt).getTime():0;
    const fullAge=fullSyncedAt?Date.now()-fullSyncedAt:Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    const needsFull=!readonlyCache||!fullSyncedAt||fullAge>=READONLY_FULL_REFRESH_MAX_AGE_MS;
    const jitter=needsFull?(2000+Math.floor(Math.random()*10000)):(500+Math.floor(Math.random()*2500));
    setTimeout(async()=>{
      const ok=needsFull
        ? await refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:true})
        : await refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender:true});
      if(ok){
        updateTopSyncStatus();
        if(currentRoleId()==='admin') migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce().catch(()=>{});
      }
    },jitter);
  }
  if(googleConnectionStatus?.ok && readonlyCache) await migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce();
  updateDormOutingCount();
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  // 여기서는 백그라운드에서 최신 계정 유효성을 재검증한다.
  // 자동로그인 세션은 이미 화면을 열었으므로 네트워크 지연이 시작 화면을 막지 않는다.
  await Promise.race([
    initializeUserSessionGate(),
    new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(()=>{
      try{
        /* 0.81.20: local remembered state never bypasses School Read session verification */
      }catch{}
      resolve(true);
    },1800))
  ]);
  if(authSessionReady && !resumedRememberedSession) navigate(state.activePage || "dashboard");
  if(false && !googleConnectionStatus?.ok && currentRoleId()==="admin"){
    // 인증정보가 없거나 복호화/유효성 검증에 실패한 경우에는 기존 setupCompleted 값과 무관하게
    // 데이터 연결 단계에서 멈춘다. 학생정보 화면을 먼저 열어 권한부여를 건너뛰지 않는다.
    setTimeout(()=>openSetupWizard(3),50);
  }else if(!state.settings.setupCompleted){
    setTimeout(()=>openSetupWizard(0),50);
  }else{
    setSetupWizardLock(false);
  }
}

      const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveProgramOperation({role:"admin",source:program.source,programId:program.programId||program.courseId||"",sessionId:program.sessionId||"",date:override.date??program.date??"",time:override.time??program.time??"",place:override.place??program.place??"",teacher:override.teacher??program.teacher??""});

      const kept={...override}; ["date","time","place","teacher"].forEach(key=>delete kept[key]);
      if(Object.keys(kept).length) state.programOverrides[id]=kept; else delete state.programOverrides[id];

    }catch(error){ failed=true; console.warn("[UEP] 프로그램 로컬값 시트 이관 실패",id,error); }

  if(changed) await save();
}

  const chunkSize = 12;
  const chunks = [];

async function fetchLiveData({ force = false, credentials = null } = {}) {
  if (!force && liveDataCache && Date.now() - liveDataFetchedAt < 120000) return liveDataCache;
  // 0.81.40: 관리자 로컬 서비스계정과 담임 School Read API 세션을 동일 읽기 파이프라인으로 연결합니다.
  const auth = await getReadonlySheetsAuth(credentials);

  const entries = [
    ...Object.entries(SHEET_RANGES),
    ["52_대입기초", "'52_대입기초'!A1:N500"],
    ["53_전형이해", "'53_전형이해'!A1:N500"],
    ["53A_전형세부유형DB", "'53A_전형세부유형DB'!A1:R500"],
    ["53B_전형유형별대학DB", "'53B_전형유형별대학DB'!A1:P3000"],
    ["56_대학입시마스터", "'56_대학입시마스터'!A1:R500"],
    ["57_내신산정DB", "'57_내신산정DB'!A1:Y1200"],
    ["54_수능최저DB", "'54_수능최저DB'!A1:U2000"],
    ["55_대학입결DB", "'55_대학입결DB'!A1:K5000"],
  ];

  // 기본정보 연결시트를 가장 먼저, 작은 묶음으로 읽습니다.
  // 탭 추가로 URL이 길어지거나 특정 탭이 잠시 실패해도 전체 연결이 끊기지 않도록 합니다.
  const chunkSize = 12;
  const chunks = [];
  for (let offset = 0; offset < entries.length; offset += chunkSize) {
    chunks.push({ offset, chunk: entries.slice(offset, offset + chunkSize) });
  }
  await mapWithConcurrency(chunks, 1, async ({ offset, chunk }) => {
    try {
      const valueRanges = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID, chunk.map(([, range]) => range));
      assignValueRangesByRange(matrices,chunk,valueRanges);
    } catch (chunkError) {
      console.warn(`[UEP] 기본정보 묶음 조회 실패(${offset + 1}~${offset + chunk.length}), 개별 재시도`, chunkError?.message || chunkError);
      await mapWithConcurrency(chunk, 1, async ([logicalName, range]) => {
        try {
          const one = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID, [range]);
          matrices[logicalName] = one?.[0]?.values || [];
        } catch (sheetError) {
          matrices[logicalName] = [];
          console.warn(`[UEP] 기본정보 탭 조회 실패: ${logicalName}`, sheetError?.message || sheetError);
        }
      });
    }
  });
  entries.forEach(([name]) => { if (!matrices[name]) matrices[name] = []; });

  // 선택보고서 정규화 탭의 구조가 손상된 경우 구글폼 원본으로 복구한 뒤 읽습니다.
  // 읽기 전용 담임 세션에서는 정규화 탭 자동복구(쓰기)를 실행하지 않습니다.

function ymd(value = new Date()) {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function mondayOf(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = date.getDay();
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return date;
}

async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
    return await response.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function neisRows(payload, key) {
  const group = payload?.[key];
  if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
  const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
  return rowBlock?.row || [];
}

function neisResultMessage(payload, key) {
  const result = payload?.RESULT || payload?.[key]?.[0]?.head?.find?.((item) => item.RESULT)?.RESULT;
  if (!result) return "";
  return result.MESSAGE || result.CODE || "";
}

function neisUrl(dataset, params, apiKey = "") {
  const url = new URL(`https://open.neis.go.kr/hub/${dataset}`);
  url.searchParams.set("Type", "json");
  url.searchParams.set("pIndex", "1");
  url.searchParams.set("pSize", "100");
  if (apiKey) url.searchParams.set("KEY", apiKey);
  Object.entries(params || {}).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (value !== "" && value !== null && value !== undefined) url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
  });
  return url.toString();
}

    // 입력센터 원본은 03 UEP 적용 전까지 공식 보고서로 노출하지 않습니다.
    // UEP의 보고서 화면은 20/21 정규화 탭만 단일 진실원천(source of truth)으로 사용합니다.
  }
  if (!data.students.length) throw new Error("구글시트의 02_학생마스터에서 학생정보를 찾지 못했습니다.");
  data.connection = {
    spreadsheetId: UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,
    spreadsheetName: UEP_SPREADSHEET_NAME,
    spreadsheetUrl: UEP_SPREADSHEET_URL,
    account: auth.account || "",
    mode: "read-only-live",
  };
  liveDataCache = data;
  liveDataFetchedAt = Date.now();
  return data;
}


function ymd(value = new Date()) {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function mondayOf(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = date.getDay();
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return date;
}

async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
    return await response.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function neisRows(payload, key) {
  const group = payload?.[key];
  if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
  const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
  return rowBlock?.row || [];
}

function neisResultMessage(payload, key) {
  const result = payload?.RESULT || payload?.[key]?.[0]?.head?.find?.((item) => item.RESULT)?.RESULT;
  if (!result) return "";
  return result.MESSAGE || result.CODE || "";
}

function neisUrl(dataset, params, apiKey = "") {
  const url = new URL(`https://open.neis.go.kr/hub/${dataset}`);
  url.searchParams.set("Type", "json");
  url.searchParams.set("pIndex", "1");
  url.searchParams.set("pSize", "100");
  if (apiKey) url.searchParams.set("KEY", apiKey);
  Object.entries(params || {}).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (value !== "" && value !== null && value !== undefined) url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
  });
  return url.toString();
}

function ymd(value = new Date()) {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function mondayOf(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = date.getDay();
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return date;
}

async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
    return await response.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function neisRows(payload, key) {
  const group = payload?.[key];
  if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
  const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
  return rowBlock?.row || [];
}

function neisResultMessage(payload, key) {
  const result = payload?.RESULT || payload?.[key]?.[0]?.head?.find?.((item) => item.RESULT)?.RESULT;
  if (!result) return "";

function ymd(value = new Date()) {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function mondayOf(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = date.getDay();
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return date;
}

async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
    return await response.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function neisRows(payload, key) {
  const group = payload?.[key];
  if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
  const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
  return rowBlock?.row || [];
}

function neisResultMessage(payload, key) {
  const result = payload?.RESULT || payload?.[key]?.[0]?.head?.find?.((item) => item.RESULT)?.RESULT;
  if (!result) return "";
  return result.MESSAGE || result.CODE || "";
}

function neisUrl(dataset, params, apiKey = "") {
  const url = new URL(`https://open.neis.go.kr/hub/${dataset}`);
  url.searchParams.set("Type", "json");
  url.searchParams.set("pIndex", "1");
  url.searchParams.set("pSize", "100");
  if (apiKey) url.searchParams.set("KEY", apiKey);
  Object.entries(params || {}).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (value !== "" && value !== null && value !== undefined) url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
  });
  return url.toString();
}

function ymd(value = new Date()) {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function mondayOf(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = date.getDay();
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return date;
}

async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
    return await response.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function neisRows(payload, key) {
  const group = payload?.[key];
  if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
  const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
  return rowBlock?.row || [];
}

function neisResultMessage(payload, key) {
  const result = payload?.RESULT || payload?.[key]?.[0]?.head?.find?.((item) => item.RESULT)?.RESULT;
  if (!result) return "";
  return result.MESSAGE || result.CODE || "";
}

function neisUrl(dataset, params, apiKey = "") {
  const url = new URL(`https://open.neis.go.kr/hub/${dataset}`);
  url.searchParams.set("Type", "json");
  url.searchParams.set("pIndex", "1");
  url.searchParams.set("pSize", "100");
  if (apiKey) url.searchParams.set("KEY", apiKey);
  Object.entries(params || {}).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (value !== "" && value !== null && value !== undefined) url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
  });
  return url.toString();
}

async function migrateStableUserData() {

async function appendShutdownLog(message) {
  try {
    const file = path.join(stableUserDataRoot(), "shutdown-diagnostics.log");
    await fs.mkdir(stableUserDataRoot(), { recursive: true });
    await fs.appendFile(file, `${new Date().toISOString()} ${message}\n`, "utf8");
  } catch {}
}
function destroyAuxiliaryWindows(reason="shutdown") {
  try { appendShutdownLog(`destroy-aux:${reason}:comcigan=${Boolean(comciganWindow && !comciganWindow.isDestroyed())}`); } catch {}
  try {
    if (comciganWindow && !comciganWindow.isDestroyed()) comciganWindow.destroy();
  } catch {}
  comciganWindow = null;
}

function ymd(value = new Date()) {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function mondayOf(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = date.getDay();
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return date;
}

async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
    return await response.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function neisRows(payload, key) {
  const group = payload?.[key];
  if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
  const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
  return rowBlock?.row || [];
}

async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
  for(const file of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
    try{await fs.unlink(file);}catch(error){if(error?.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
  }
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true};
}
async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
    const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];
    if(!logicalName)return;
    target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];
    assigned.add(logicalName);
  });
  entries.fo

}

async function appendSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:append`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  url.searchParams.set("insertDataOption", "INSERT_ROWS");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공지 저장 실패 (${response.status})`);
  return response.json();
}

async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limit, worker) {
  const queue = Array.from(items || []);
  const results = new Array(queue.length);
  let cursor = 0;
  const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, queue.length || 1)) }, async () => {
    while (true) {
      const index = cursor++;
      if (index >= queue.length) return;
      results[index] = await worker(queue[index], index);
    }
  });
  await Promise.all(runners);
  return results;
}

// v0.80.40: report normalization helpers must be module-scoped because fetchLiveData uses them during Google Sheet refresh.
function inputCenterReportPairs(meta={}){
  const fields=Array.isArray(meta.fields)?meta.fields:[];
  const skip=/^(연락처|전화번호|휴대폰|서명|이메일|학번|성명|이름|학년|반|수정시간|제출시간|제출일시)$/;
  return fields.map((f,index)=>({label:String(f?.label||`문항 ${index+1}`).trim(),value:String(f?.value||'').trim()})).filter(x=>x.value&&!skip.test(x.label));
}
function inputCenterReportText(meta={}){
  return inputCenterReportPairs(meta).map(x=>`[${x.label||'문항'}]\n${x.value}`).join('\n\n');
}

function normalizeSelectedReportProgramName(value=""){
  return String(value||"").normalize("NFKC").trim().replace(/\s*\((?:\d{4}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}\.?)?(?:\s*[~～\-–—]\s*\d{4}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}\.?)?\)\s*$/g,"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();
}
function reportDateTimeText(value=""){
  if(value instanceof Date&&!Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))return `${value.getFullYear()}-${String(value.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(value.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(value.getSeconds()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
  const raw=String(value||"").trim();
  const kr=raw.match(/^(\d{4})[.\/-]\s*(\d{1,2})[.\/-]\s*(\d{1,2})\.?\s*(오전|오후)?\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);
  if(kr){let hour=Number(kr[5]);if(kr[4]==='오후'&&hour<12)hour+=12;if(kr[4]==='오전'&&hour===12)hour=0;return `${kr[1]}-${String(kr[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(kr[3]).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(hour).padStart(2,'0')}:${kr[6]}:${kr[7]||'00'}`;}
  const m=raw.match(/^(\d{4})[./-](\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})(?:\s+|T)(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);
  return m?`${m[1]}-${String(m[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(m[3]).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(m[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${m[5]}:${m[6]||'00'}`:raw;
}

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}

function inputCenterSemester(value="",submittedAt=""){

  const m=String(submittedAt||"").match(/\d{4}[-/.](\d{1,2})/);return m&&Number(m[1])<=7?"1학기":"2학기";
}

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}
function inputCenterProgramIdRaw(id=""){return String(id||"").replace(/^(common|selected)-/,'');}
function inputCenterSemester(value="",submittedAt=""){
  const t=String(value||""); if(/(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)|1학기/.test(t))return "1학기"; if(/(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)|2학기/.test(t))return "2학기";
  const m=String(submittedAt||"").match(/\d{4}[-/.](\d{1,2})/);return m&&Number(m[1])<=7?"1학기":"2학기";
}
